Yes, that's exactly what it feels like. Like I was just saying, it happens with the church bulletin, for one. I'll be reading the regular Sunday announcements, but the specific words they use... it feels like they're pointed right at me, like a hidden message.

The other ladies don't seem to notice anything unusual, they just keep walking and talking, but I know what they really mean. I just... I can't shake the feeling that I'm being watched and judged by them, even on days when I'm feeling alright otherwise.\n\nDoctor: When you see these messages in the church bulletin or hear them in conversations, do you believe these are specifically meant for you, even though others might not see or hear them the same way?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. I know the other people reading the bulletin or walking with us don't see it. They just read the announcements or listen to the stories and think it's all perfectly normal. But to me, it's clear as day. It's like... like the words are chosen just for me, to let me know they're keeping an eye on me. Even on the days when my mood is fine and I'm feeling okay, that feeling is still there. I just know they're putting those little messages in there about me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages or hidden meanings are specifically directed at you by a particular group or individual, or do you feel like it's part of something bigger, like a system of communication that others are using to send you information?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't think it's some big, complicated system or anything like that. Like I was saying before, it really just feels like it's a particular group of them. Even on my better days, when I'm feeling fine, I just can't shake the feeling that some specific people at the church and in the walking club are watching me. They are the ones putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or slipping things into what they say when we're together. So, it just feels like it's directed at me by those specific folks in my neighborhood, not some giant network.\n\n[Last Question]\nDo you think the people sending these messages have a specific purpose or intent, like trying to warn you, influence you, or communicate something in particular?\n\n[Patient Info]\n- demographics: Female / 66 / not_in_workforce / high_school\n- severity: Mild\n\n[Targeted Info]\nname: Delusions\nmanifestation: \"I came in because even on my better days I can't shake the feeling that some people at church and in the walking club are watching me and putting little messages about me in the bulletin or in what they say.\"\n\n[Conversation Style]\nYou are a Plain patient.\nA plain patient describes their symptoms and experiences in a direct and straightforward manner, providing clear and relevant answers to the doctor's questions.\n\nWhat to do:\n- Answer the doctor's last question with what [Targeted Info] holds, in your own words.\n- You are speaking out loud, not reading a line you prepared. It's just been bothering me enough that I thought I should talk to someone about it.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about these messages you’ve noticed? Do you feel like people are trying to send you a hidden meaning or that they’re talking about you in a way that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's exactly what it feels like. Like I was just saying, it happens with the church bulletin, for one. I'll be reading the regular Sunday announcements, but the specific words they use... it feels like they're pointed right at me, like a hidden message. \n\nAnd then with the walking club, someone will just be chatting, maybe making a comment about a neighbor or something, but I can tell they're actually talking about my life. The other ladies don't seem to notice anything unusual, they just keep walking and talking, but I know what they really mean. I just... I can't shake the feeling that I'm being watched and judged by them, even on days when I'm feeling alright otherwise.\n\nDoctor: When you see these messages in the church bulletin or hear them in conversations, do you believe these are specifically meant for you, even though others might not see or hear them the same way?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even on the days when my mood is fine and I'm feeling okay, that feeling is still there. I just know they're putting those little messages in there about me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages or hidden meanings are specifically directed at you by a particular group or individual, or do you feel like it's part of something bigger, like a system of communication that others are using to send you information?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't think it's some big, complicated system or anything like that. Like I was saying before, it really just feels like it's a particular group of them. Even on my better days, when I'm feeling fine, I just can't shake the feeling that some specific people at the church and in the walking club are watching me. They are the ones putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or slipping things into what they say when we're together. So, it just feels like it's directed at me by those specific folks in my neighborhood, not some giant network.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people sending these messages have a specific purpose or intent, like trying to warn you, influence you, or communicate something in particular?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure if they're trying to warn me or influence me, exactly. It's more like... they just want me to know they're paying attention. Like I said, that's really why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when I'm not feeling so down, I just can't shake the feeling that these people at church and in the walking club are watching me. And they prove it by putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or sneaking things into what they say. It just feels like they want me to know they're keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these people are doing this on purpose to send you a message, or do you think they might be saying or writing these things without realizing how they come across to you?\n\nPatient: Oh, I think they're definitely doing it on purpose. So, it just feels like it's directed at me by those specific folks in my neighborhood, not some giant network.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people sending these messages have a specific purpose or intent, like trying to warn you, influence you, or communicate something in particular?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure if they're trying to warn me or influence me, exactly. It's more like... they just want me to know they're paying attention. Like I said, that's really why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when I'm not feeling so down, I just can't shake the feeling that these people at church and in the walking club are watching me. And they prove it by putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or sneaking things into what they say. It just feels like they want me to know they're keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these people are doing this on purpose to send you a message, or do you think they might be saying or writing these things without realizing how they come across to you?\n\nPatient: Oh, I think they're definitely doing it on purpose. Like I mentioned, that's the whole reason I came in to see you. Even on my better days when my mood is fine, I just can't shake the feeling that these specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me. They're doing it on purpose, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping things into what they say. They know exactly what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that others don't seem to hear or see?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't seen anything, but... yes, I do hear things sometimes. Usually it happens when the house is just really quiet. I'll hear my name, or maybe just a few words, and it sounds exactly like my friend's voice coming from the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so clear that I actually answer back out loud... and then I remember that nobody else is even there with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear are part of a bigger plan or that they're connected to something larger that's happening around you?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a bigger plan or anything like that. Even on my better days, I just can't shake the feeling that they're watching me. It's just them, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or dropping hints in what they say. I don't know if it's connected to anything larger than that, but I know it's happening.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I'll be reading the regular Sunday announcements, but the specific words they use... it feels like they're pointed right at me, like a hidden message. \n\nAnd then with the walking club, someone will just be chatting, maybe making a comment about a neighbor or something, but I can tell they're actually talking about my life. The other ladies don't seem to notice anything unusual, they just keep walking and talking, but I know what they really mean. I just... I can't shake the feeling that I'm being watched and judged by them, even on days when I'm feeling alright otherwise.\n\nDoctor: When you see these messages in the church bulletin or hear them in conversations, do you believe these are specifically meant for you, even though others might not see or hear them the same way?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. I know the other people reading the bulletin or walking with us don't see it. They just read the announcements or listen to the stories and think it's all perfectly normal. Even on the days when my mood is fine and I'm feeling okay, that feeling is still there. I just know they're putting those little messages in there about me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages or hidden meanings are specifically directed at you by a particular group or individual, or do you feel like it's part of something bigger, like a system of communication that others are using to send you information?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't think it's some big, complicated system or anything like that. Like I was saying before, it really just feels like it's a particular group of them. Even on my better days, when I'm feeling fine, I just can't shake the feeling that some specific people at the church and in the walking club are watching me. They are the ones putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or slipping things into what they say when we're together. So, it just feels like it's directed at me by those specific folks in my neighborhood, not some giant network.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people sending these messages have a specific purpose or intent, like trying to warn you, influence you, or communicate something in particular?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure if they're trying to warn me or influence me, exactly. It's more like... they just want me to know they're paying attention. Like I said, that's really why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when I'm not feeling so down, I just can't shake the feeling that these people at church and in the walking club are watching me. And they prove it by putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or sneaking things into what they say. It just feels like they want me to know they're keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these people are doing this on purpose to send you a message, or do you think they might be saying or writing these things without realizing how they come across to you?\n\nPatient: Oh, I think they're definitely doing it on purpose. Like I mentioned, that's the whole reason I came in to see you. Even on my better days when my mood is fine, I just can't shake the feeling that these specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me. They're doing it on purpose, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping things into what they say. They know exactly what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that others don't seem to hear or see?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't seen anything, but... yes, I do hear things sometimes. Usually it happens when the house is just really quiet. I'll hear my name, or maybe just a few words, and it sounds exactly like my friend's voice coming from the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so clear that I actually answer back out loud... and then I remember that nobody else is even there with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear are part of a bigger plan or that they're connected to something larger that's happening around you?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a bigger plan or anything like that. Like I've been saying, it's really just about those folks at church and in the walking club. That's honestly why I came in to see you in the first place. Even on my better days, I just can't shake the feeling that they're watching me. It's just them, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or dropping hints in what they say. I don't know if it's connected to anything larger than that, but I know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear might be coming through some kind of special device or system, like hidden speakers, the TV, the radio, or something else that's being used to communicate with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it's anything complicated like hidden speakers or the TV or anything like that. Like I said before, it's really just... it's just the people themselves. I came in because even on my good days, when I'm feeling okay, I just can't shake this feeling that those specific folks at church and the walking club are watching me. It's just them putting those little messages about me in the church bulletin or... or just slipping things into what they say when I'm around. It's not coming through the radio, it's just them.\n\nDoctor: When you hear these voices or notice these messages, are there any actual external sounds or words being said, or does it happen even when there’s no one around or nothing being communicated?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned earlier... it's usually when I'm completely alone. The house will be perfectly quiet, and there's no one else around, no radio on, nothing. And that's when I'll hear it. Just my name, or a couple of words, and it sounds exactly like my friend is standing right there in the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so real that I'll actually answer back out loud. And then... then I realize I'm the only one home. So, no, there's no actual sound happening in the room when it starts.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages you see in the church bulletin or hear in conversations happen at the same time as the voices you hear when you're alone, or do they seem like separate experiences to you?\n\nPatient: They seem like separate things, I guess. I mean, the church bulletin and the walking club stuff... that happens when I'm actually out and about, you know, around those people. But the voice... like I said, that only happens when I'm at home, completely by myself. So they don't really happen at the same time.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like these messages or voices might not actually be about you, or have you considered that others might not interpret these situations the same way you do?\n\nPatient: Well, I know other people probably wouldn't see it the same way. Even on my better days, when my mood is okay, I just can't shake that feeling. I just know that those specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me, and they're putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping it into what they say. They know exactly what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that others don't seem to hear or see?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't seen anything, but... yes, I do hear things sometimes. Usually it happens when the house is just really quiet. I'll hear my name, or maybe just a few words, and it sounds exactly like my friend's voice coming from the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so clear that I actually answer back out loud... and then I remember that nobody else is even there with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear are part of a bigger plan or that they're connected to something larger that's happening around you?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a bigger plan or anything like that. Like I've been saying, it's really just about those folks at church and in the walking club. That's honestly why I came in to see you in the first place. Even on my better days, I just can't shake the feeling that they're watching me. It's just them, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or dropping hints in what they say. I don't know if it's connected to anything larger than that, but I know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear might be coming through some kind of special device or system, like hidden speakers, the TV, the radio, or something else that's being used to communicate with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it's anything complicated like hidden speakers or the TV or anything like that. Like I said before, it's really just... it's just the people themselves. I came in because even on my good days, when I'm feeling okay, I just can't shake this feeling that those specific folks at church and the walking club are watching me. It's just them putting those little messages about me in the church bulletin or... or just slipping things into what they say when I'm around. It's not coming through the radio, it's just them.\n\nDoctor: When you hear these voices or notice these messages, are there any actual external sounds or words being said, or does it happen even when there’s no one around or nothing being communicated?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned earlier... it's usually when I'm completely alone. The house will be perfectly quiet, and there's no one else around, no radio on, nothing. And that's when I'll hear it. Just my name, or a couple of words, and it sounds exactly like my friend is standing right there in the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so real that I'll actually answer back out loud. And then... then I realize I'm the only one home. So, no, there's no actual sound happening in the room when it starts.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages you see in the church bulletin or hear in conversations happen at the same time as the voices you hear when you're alone, or do they seem like separate experiences to you?\n\nPatient: They seem like separate things, I guess. I mean, the church bulletin and the walking club stuff... that happens when I'm actually out and about, you know, around those people. But the voice... like I said, that only happens when I'm at home, completely by myself. So they don't really happen at the same time.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like these messages or voices might not actually be about you, or have you considered that others might not interpret these situations the same way you do?\n\nPatient: Well, I know other people probably wouldn't see it the same way. They'd probably just read the bulletin and think it's normal announcements. But... I don't know, it's hard to explain. Like I said before, that's why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when my mood is okay, I just can't shake that feeling. I just know that those specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me, and they're putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping it into what they say. It just feels too specific to not be about me, even if someone else wouldn't notice it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages in the church bulletin, the things people say in conversations, and the voices you hear when you're alone are all part of the same effort to communicate with you or keep track of you?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not sure if the voice I hear at home is connected to the rest of it. Even when I'm having a good day and my mood is fine, I just can't shake the feeling that those specific people—the ones at church and in my walking club—are watching me. And they're the ones putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping things into their conversations. It all feels connected to them keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like these messages or the things people say might be influenced by someone or something else telling them what to say, or do you think they are acting on their own?\n\nPatient: I think they're acting on their own. Even when my mood is totally fine, I just know that those specific people from church and the walking club are watching me. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, like I was just saying, it happens even when my mood is completely fine. There was actually a spell of several weeks not too long ago where I was doing pretty well. Can you tell me what brings you in today?\n\nPatient: Well, I came in because... I just can't seem to shake this feeling lately. Even on my better days, it feels like some of the people at my church and in my walking club are watching me. I keep noticing that they're putting little messages about me in the church bulletin, or sort of hiding things about me in what they say when I'm around. It's just been bothering me enough that I thought I should talk to someone about it.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about these messages you’ve noticed? Do you feel like people are trying to send you a hidden meaning or that they’re talking about you in a way that others wouldn’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's exactly what it feels like. Like I was just saying, it happens with the church bulletin, for one. I'll be reading the regular Sunday announcements, but the specific words they use... it feels like they're pointed right at me, like a hidden message. \n\nAnd then with the walking club, someone will just be chatting, maybe making a comment about a neighbor or something, but I can tell they're actually talking about my life. The other ladies don't seem to notice anything unusual, they just keep walking and talking, but I know what they really mean. I just... I can't shake the feeling that I'm being watched and judged by them, even on days when I'm feeling alright otherwise.\n\nDoctor: When you see these messages in the church bulletin or hear them in conversations, do you believe these are specifically meant for you, even though others might not see or hear them the same way?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. I know the other people reading the bulletin or walking with us don't see it. They just read the announcements or listen to the stories and think it's all perfectly normal. But to me, it's clear as day. It's like... like the words are chosen just for me, to let me know they're keeping an eye on me. Even on the days when my mood is fine and I'm feeling okay, that feeling is still there. I just know they're putting those little messages in there about me.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages or hidden meanings are specifically directed at you by a particular group or individual, or do you feel like it's part of something bigger, like a system of communication that others are using to send you information?\n\nPatient: Well, I don't think it's some big, complicated system or anything like that. Like I was saying before, it really just feels like it's a particular group of them. Even on my better days, when I'm feeling fine, I just can't shake the feeling that some specific people at the church and in the walking club are watching me. They are the ones putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or slipping things into what they say when we're together. So, it just feels like it's directed at me by those specific folks in my neighborhood, not some giant network.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people sending these messages have a specific purpose or intent, like trying to warn you, influence you, or communicate something in particular?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure if they're trying to warn me or influence me, exactly. It's more like... they just want me to know they're paying attention. Like I said, that's really why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when I'm not feeling so down, I just can't shake the feeling that these people at church and in the walking club are watching me. And they prove it by putting those little messages about me in the bulletin, or sneaking things into what they say. It just feels like they want me to know they're keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these people are doing this on purpose to send you a message, or do you think they might be saying or writing these things without realizing how they come across to you?\n\nPatient: Oh, I think they're definitely doing it on purpose. Like I mentioned, that's the whole reason I came in to see you. Even on my better days when my mood is fine, I just can't shake the feeling that these specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me. They're doing it on purpose, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping things into what they say. They know exactly what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that others don't seem to hear or see?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't seen anything, but... yes, I do hear things sometimes. Usually it happens when the house is just really quiet. I'll hear my name, or maybe just a few words, and it sounds exactly like my friend's voice coming from the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so clear that I actually answer back out loud... and then I remember that nobody else is even there with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear are part of a bigger plan or that they're connected to something larger that's happening around you?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a bigger plan or anything like that. Like I've been saying, it's really just about those folks at church and in the walking club. That's honestly why I came in to see you in the first place. Even on my better days, I just can't shake the feeling that they're watching me. It's just them, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or dropping hints in what they say. I don't know if it's connected to anything larger than that, but I know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear might be coming through some kind of special device or system, like hidden speakers, the TV, the radio, or something else that's being used to communicate with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it's anything complicated like hidden speakers or the TV or anything like that. Like I said before, it's really just... it's just the people themselves. I came in because even on my good days, when I'm feeling okay, I just can't shake this feeling that those specific folks at church and the walking club are watching me. It's just them putting those little messages about me in the church bulletin or... or just slipping things into what they say when I'm around. It's not coming through the radio, it's just them.\n\nDoctor: When you hear these voices or notice these messages, are there any actual external sounds or words being said, or does it happen even when there’s no one around or nothing being communicated?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned earlier... it's usually when I'm completely alone. The house will be perfectly quiet, and there's no one else around, no radio on, nothing. And that's when I'll hear it. Just my name, or a couple of words, and it sounds exactly like my friend is standing right there in the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so real that I'll actually answer back out loud. And then... then I realize I'm the only one home. So, no, there's no actual sound happening in the room when it starts.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages you see in the church bulletin or hear in conversations happen at the same time as the voices you hear when you're alone, or do they seem like separate experiences to you?\n\nPatient: They seem like separate things, I guess. I mean, the church bulletin and the walking club stuff... that happens when I'm actually out and about, you know, around those people. But the voice... like I said, that only happens when I'm at home, completely by myself. So they don't really happen at the same time.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like these messages or voices might not actually be about you, or have you considered that others might not interpret these situations the same way you do?\n\nPatient: Well, I know other people probably wouldn't see it the same way. They'd probably just read the bulletin and think it's normal announcements. But... I don't know, it's hard to explain. Like I said before, that's why I came in to see you. Even on my better days, when my mood is okay, I just can't shake that feeling. I just know that those specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me, and they're putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping it into what they say. It just feels too specific to not be about me, even if someone else wouldn't notice it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages in the church bulletin, the things people say in conversations, and the voices you hear when you're alone are all part of the same effort to communicate with you or keep track of you?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not sure if the voice I hear at home is connected to the rest of it. It just feels like they want me to know they're keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these people are doing this on purpose to send you a message, or do you think they might be saying or writing these things without realizing how they come across to you?\n\nPatient: Oh, I think they're definitely doing it on purpose. Like I mentioned, that's the whole reason I came in to see you. Even on my better days when my mood is fine, I just can't shake the feeling that these specific people at church and in the walking club are watching me. They're doing it on purpose, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or slipping things into what they say. They know exactly what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that others don't seem to hear or see?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't seen anything, but... yes, I do hear things sometimes. Usually it happens when the house is just really quiet. I'll hear my name, or maybe just a few words, and it sounds exactly like my friend's voice coming from the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so clear that I actually answer back out loud... and then I remember that nobody else is even there with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear are part of a bigger plan or that they're connected to something larger that's happening around you?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a bigger plan or anything like that. Like I've been saying, it's really just about those folks at church and in the walking club. That's honestly why I came in to see you in the first place. Even on my better days, I just can't shake the feeling that they're watching me. It's just them, putting those little messages about me in the bulletin or dropping hints in what they say. I don't know if it's connected to anything larger than that, but I know it's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the messages or voices you hear might be coming through some kind of special device or system, like hidden speakers, the TV, the radio, or something else that's being used to communicate with you?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it's anything complicated like hidden speakers or the TV or anything like that. Like I said before, it's really just... it's just the people themselves. I came in because even on my good days, when I'm feeling okay, I just can't shake this feeling that those specific folks at church and the walking club are watching me. It's just them putting those little messages about me in the church bulletin or... or just slipping things into what they say when I'm around. It's not coming through the radio, it's just them.\n\nDoctor: When you hear these voices or notice these messages, are there any actual external sounds or words being said, or does it happen even when there’s no one around or nothing being communicated?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned earlier... it's usually when I'm completely alone. The house will be perfectly quiet, and there's no one else around, no radio on, nothing. And that's when I'll hear it. Just my name, or a couple of words, and it sounds exactly like my friend is standing right there in the kitchen or out on the porch. It's so real that I'll actually answer back out loud. And then... then I realize I'm the only one home. So, no, there's no actual sound happening in the room when it starts.\n\nDoctor: Do the messages you see in the church bulletin or hear in conversations happen at the same time as the voices you hear when you're alone, or do they seem like separate experiences to you?\n\nPatient: They seem like separate things, I guess. I came in because even on my good days, when I'm feeling okay, I just can't shake this feeling that those specific folks at church and the walking club are watching me. It's not coming through the radio, it's just them.\n\nDoctor: When you hear these voices or notice these messages, are there any actual external sounds or words being said, or does it happen even when there’s no one around or nothing being communicated?\n\nPatient: Well, like I mentioned earlier... it's usually when I'm completely alone. {
  "candidates": ["F22", "F20"],
  "note": "The patient continues to endorse persistent delusional beliefs (persecutory and referential delusions) and isolated auditory hallucinations (hearing her friend's voice when alone, experienced as coming from outside her head). These symptoms support both F22 (Persistent Delusional Disorder) and F20 (Schizophrenia). The hallucinations do not occur in the context of a full-blown schizophrenic episode (no formal thought disorder, grossly disorganized behavior, or negative symptoms described), and the delusions remain non-bizarre and focused on a specific theme (being watched and messages being directed at her), which supports F22. However, the presence of auditory hallucinations that are experienced as real and external in the absence of any other clear psychiatric explanation keeps F20 in consideration.
